Podcast Picks — Breakdown by Show

Last updated November 08, 2026, 09:41 AM ET · Source: Podcast Picks Tracker Google Sheet · 2584 picks logged · 2035 graded (1029-975-31) · Graded P/L +$1,021.52 at $100 flat · Paper inverse 896-1013-31, -$21,229.27
How to read this page. Every pick below shows the price it was logged at — that column is bold, and any pick at +200 or longer is highlighted in gold. Prices drive a big share of the damage (see the price section below). Paper inverse = fading only the invertible picks (spread / ML / totals / prop over-unders / alt lines) at modeled reverse prices — the opposite side priced by flipping the implied probability and adding standard -110/-110 juice. It is paper-only, never real-money advice. Note on names: the tracker logs Gill and Gil Alexander as one host, and Kelley / Kelly Bydlon as one host. John Hanson (Prop Points host) and John Hansen (guest) are two different people. Alex White is a Circa Friday Football Invitational contestant — any picks under her name are logged under that show (see its section below), not under a host show. The A Numbers Game host with the strongest record is Gill Alexander (17-12-0, +$299.13).
Why both sides lose: the regular picks win 1029 of 2004 decided (51.3%) and the blanket fade wins 896 of 1909 decided (46.9%). Both sit inside the band where the juice eats both sides — following needs >52.4% at -110 to profit, and so does fading at modeled reverse prices. The only fade that clearly works is Jensen Lewis on By The Books: +$1,303.53 fading him (64-42-1). The worst fade is Alex White on Circa Friday Football Invitational: -$2,245.44 — fading a winner loses.
